# State management refactor

**Status:** planned · **Owner:** POM · **Last updated:** 2026-04-15 (v3.1 — lean, corrected)

## Why

Two observable bugs we can't patch:
- Model Select flashes closed on click.
- Prompt textarea rejects typing.

Both caused by 15–20 cascading renders on `ImageLightbox` open. Root cause: shared client state lives in 4–5 layers of hooks + contexts, each returning unstable object references, cascading re-renders that unmount Base UI overlay portals before click-outside detection settles.

Patching individual unstable returns doesn't hold — the architecture regenerates the bug elsewhere. Fix: subscription-based store (Zustand). Consumers subscribe to specific slices; unrelated updates don't cascade; overlay portals stay mounted.

## Non-goals

- Replacing React Query (it stays as the server layer).
- Changing tool registration (`defineTool()` deferred indefinitely).
- SSR, bundler, or Supabase changes.
- Parallel execution across multiple agents.

## Tech choice

**Zustand.** Rejected: Jotai (higher cognitive load), Redux Toolkit (ceremony), Valtio (mutable proxies), `useSyncExternalStore` directly (lower uniformity for future contributors).

## Architecture — the decisions locked in prose

- **One store per domain, entity-keyed**: `{entities: Record<entityId, T>}`. Single store for settings, single for selection, etc.
- **One-way data flow**: store is the write source of truth; RQ invalidates the store's cache key on successful save. No two-way binding.
- **Selector equality**: `shallow` for objects/arrays, default `Object.is` for primitives.
- **Overlay scope** (M2): every Base UI portal primitive — `Dialog`, `Popover`, `Select`, `Menu`, `Tooltip-with-modal`, `Drawer`, `AlertDialog`, `ContextMenu`.
- **Hook API stability**: migrate internals, keep signatures unless a hook is a 1:1 passthrough — then delete it and let callers use selectors directly.

## Prerequisites (one-day block before M0)

1. `megaplan-harness` v0.13: add `--phase-timeout SECONDS` to the `auto` driver. (Half day on the megaplan repo.)
2. OpenAI spending cap → $150/month.
3. Fine-grained GitHub PAT scoped to `banodoco/reigh-app` only, contents + PR read/write, replaces the classic PAT.
4. Branch protection on `main`: require PR, no force-push.

## Milestones

### M0 — Telemetry + baseline (1 day)

Add `src/shared/dev/useRenderBudget.ts` + `RenderTelemetryOverlay.tsx`. Instrument the highest-traffic surfaces (grep component names in the repo first — this list is a starting point, not authoritative):
- `ImageLightbox`, `EditModePanel`, `LightboxShell`
- `TimelineCanvas` and one representative timeline track/clip component (verify name in repo; earlier drafts used `ClipRow` which does not exist)
- `MediaGalleryItem`
- `TasksPane`, `ShotsPanelContent` (at `src/features/editor/components/`), `GenerationsPaneGallery`
- `PreviewPanel`

Record baseline counts in commit messages for 3 interactions each (lightbox open, clip select, pane toggle).

**Done when:** telemetry works in dev, tree-shakes in prod build, baseline numbers committed.

### M1 — Foundation + first port (3–4 days)

`npm install zustand`. Write `src/shared/state/createEntityStore.ts`. Port `useJoinClipsSettings.ts` as the smallest consumer to validate factory shape — public return unchanged.

Factory API:
```typescript
function createEntityStore<T extends object>(config: {
  toolId: string;
  defaults: T;
  load: (entityId: string) => Promise<{ db: T | null; lastUsed: T | null }>;
  save: (entityId: string, data: T) => Promise<void>;
  textFieldKeys?: readonly (keyof T)[];  // flush on blur, not on debounce
  persistenceDebounceMs?: number;         // default 300
}): {
  useEntity(entityId: string): EntityView<T>;
  // actions: bootstrapEntity, updateField, updateFields, updateTextField,
  //          flushTextFields, saveImmediate, revert, reset
};
```

**Done when:** `useJoinClipsSettings` tests pass, typecheck clean, factory is generic.

### M1a — Settings store rollout (4–6 days)

Rewrite `useAutoSaveSettings.ts` over the factory. Delete `useDebouncedSettingsSave.ts`, `autoSaveSettingsHelpers.ts`, `autoSaveSettingsLoaders.ts`. Kill the render-time state mutation in `useEditSettingsPersistence.ts:110-114` and the `hasInitializedRef` state machine. Kill the ref-as-state pattern in `useLastUsedEditSettings.ts`.

Migrate direct callers surfaced by:
```
rg 'from .*useAutoSaveSettings' src/ --type ts -l
```
(Expect ~13 production callers plus tests. Run at milestone start, paste the list into the PR. Note: hooks like `useEditSettingsPersistence` and `useInlineEditState` are **transitive** — they consume `useAutoSaveSettings` indirectly via other hooks — not direct callers. The grep is the truth; if count is wildly different, halt.)

**MUST:**
- `ImageLightbox` opens with ≤ 5 renders (≥ 75% under M0 baseline).
- `EditModePanel` ≤ 3 renders on lightbox open.
- Model Select stays open + accepts selection.
- Textarea accepts typing without lost keystrokes.
- Typecheck clean, test suite matches M0 baseline.
- **Dependency audit of `useImageLightboxEnvironment`**: every hook feeding the env object (`useLoraManager`, `useUpscale`, `useEditSettingsPersistence`, `usePanes`, `useUserUIState`, etc.) must return a stable reference across renders when inputs haven't changed. Verify by instrumenting the env with a ref-equality logger in dev mode. Any unstable return blocks the gate — fix locally (memoize) or expand M1a's scope to include the offending hook.

**Why the audit:** the motivating bug is caused by `useImageLightboxEnvironment` returning a new object reference per render. `useAutoSaveSettings` is one input among many. Fixing only that path doesn't guarantee the bug is gone — a different unstable input can keep the storm alive. This gate check prevents declaring victory prematurely.

**Decision point (gate):** If the original bug is fixed AND the dependency audit passes → **skip M2 entirely**; M2 becomes optional. If the bug still reproduces after the audit → run M2.

### M1b — Selection store (5–7 days)

Collapse into `src/shared/state/selectionStore.ts` with slices `gallery`, `timeline`, `shot`:
- `src/shared/contexts/GallerySelectionContext.tsx`
- `src/shared/contexts/LastAffectedShotContext.tsx`
- `src/shared/contexts/CurrentShotContext.tsx`
- `src/shared/contexts/ShotAdditionSelectionContext.tsx`
- Selection state inside `src/tools/video-editor/hooks/useTimelineState.ts`

Cross-context effect-chain syncs become store actions. Discover the behaviors by using the app — don't write a matrix up front.

**MUST:** every selection-dependent UX behavior (gallery ↔ timeline ↔ shot) preserved; per-domain renders confined to that domain; typecheck + tests at baseline.

**Decision point (gate):** If regressions cluster in one area → re-scope next milestone to fix root cause before continuing. Otherwise continue to M1c.

### M1c — Timeline editor store (7–10 days, high-risk)

Paths corrected from v1: `TimelineEditorData` and `Ops` live in one file (`src/tools/video-editor/contexts/TimelineEditorContext.tsx`). The timeline store lands at `src/tools/video-editor/hooks/timelineStore.ts` (NOT `state/`).

Merge: `TimelineEditorContext` (Data + Ops), `TimelineChromeContext`, `TimelinePlaybackContext` into one store with slices.

Port `useTimelineState.ts`. Sub-hooks (`useClipDrag`, `useClipResizeGesture`, `useAssetManagement`, `useExternalDrop`, `useTimelinePersistence`, `usePollSync`) keep their own files + control flow — they read/write the store but own their state machines.

**`VideoEditorProvider` is bigger than the 4 contexts.** It also owns: AgentChat bridge registration (`AgentChatBridgeRegistration` + related hooks), the pending-add / `ADD_GENERATION_QUERY_PARAM` flow that drains on mount (the feature we built earlier this session), `onDoubleClickAsset`, `navResult` from `useVideoEditorLightboxNavigation`, and lightbox state (`lightboxAssetKey`, `lightboxClipId`, `lightboxQuery`, `lightboxFallbackMedia`). Pre-flight: inventory everything in `VideoEditorProvider.tsx` and decide per-item: migrate to store, keep as local provider state, or extract to a sibling hook. Don't delete those pathways silently.

**Critical:** some `editorRef.current` / `dataRef` refs are stability cope (safe to delete with the store landing); others guard pointer handlers against stale closures (load-bearing). Classify during the migration, don't pre-classify.

**MUST:**
- Scroll: `TimelineCanvas` ≤ 2 renders per scroll event.
- Clip drag: only the dragged clip/row component re-renders during the drag; other rows at 0. (Clip-row component name to be confirmed at M0 instrumentation — earlier drafts referenced `ClipRow` which does not exist in the repo.)
- All keyboard shortcuts, selection, playback, drag, resize work identically.
- AgentChat bridge + pending-add-from-URL flow still work post-migration.
- Typecheck + tests at baseline.

**Flagged experimental.** Expect to hand-finish drag/resize physics even if codex returns a working-looking PR. Budget accordingly.

**Decision point (gate):** If drag physics regress in ways codex can't fix in 1 revise cycle → revert to branch, iterate manually. Don't auto-accept working-tests-that-feel-janky.

### M2 — Overlay stack (3–5 days, CONDITIONAL)

**Only if M1a's gate shows the bug still reproduces.** Otherwise skip.

`src/shared/state/overlayStack.ts` + primitives in `src/shared/components/ui/overlay/`. Wrap every Base UI portal primitive per AD-4 (Dialog, Popover, Select, Menu, Tooltip-with-modal, Drawer, AlertDialog, ContextMenu). Body `pointer-events: none` owned by the outermost open overlay only. Focus restoration centralized.

Inventory: `rg 'from .*@base-ui/react/(dialog|popover|select|menu|tooltip|alert-dialog|context-menu|popup)' src/ --type ts -l`. Expect 20+ callsites.

**MUST:** original bug repro (open LoRA modal → close → open Model Select) stays open; integration test + manual smoke assert `document.body.style.pointerEvents === ''` after every modeled overlay close. (No browser E2E harness exists in the repo today — integration tests at RTL/Vitest level cover the contract; a true E2E harness is out of scope.)

### M3 — Realtime events store (2–3 days)

`src/shared/state/realtimeStore.ts` — slice per task keyed by `taskId`. Rewrite `RealtimeProvider`'s broadcast; keep the provider as a RQ → store mount. Migrate the real task-state surfaces (verify names at milestone start):
- `src/shared/hooks/tasks/useTaskStatusCounts.ts`
- `src/shared/hooks/tasks/usePendingGenerationTasks.ts`
- `src/shared/components/TaskDetails/hooks/useGenerationTaskDetails.ts`
- Any other consumer surfaced by `rg 'useTaskStatus|usePendingGenerationTasks|useGenerationTaskDetails|RealtimeProvider' src/ --type ts -l`.

Also inventory adjacent task-state paths before coding: global task providers, incoming-placeholder state, generation-task mapping cache, aggregate counts used by UI. Decide what becomes store-backed vs. what stays as-is — not everything needs to migrate.

**MUST:** gallery stops flickering on unrelated task events; `useTaskStatus(id)` only re-renders for that task (verify with telemetry).

### M4 — Panes + context audit (3 days)

`src/shared/state/panesStore.ts`. Delete `PanesContext`. Audit remaining `createContext` with >10 field values; port if consumers >5, document otherwise. Add ESLint rule against fat contexts going forward.

**MUST:** opening one pane doesn't re-render other panes; mobile exclusive-pane behavior preserved.

### M5 — Test triage (1–3 days)

Run the full suite after M4. Fix real regressions, delete implementation-coupled tests that didn't survive the refactor. Expect delete count > fix count.

**MUST:** `npm test` exits 0.

### M6 — Behavior tests (2–3 days)

Convert 10 implementation-coupled tests to `@testing-library/react` user-interaction tests. Add 2 E2E-style regression tests for the original bugs: Model Select sequence, textarea typing sequence.

## Order

Serial, one milestone per week at pace:

```
Prereqs → M0 → M1 → M1a → [M2 if needed] → M1b → M1c → M3 → M4 → M5 → M6
```

M2 is conditional per M1a's outcome. M3 could technically parallel M1b (same factory, disjoint files), but we run serial for review capacity.

## Safety

- `megaplan auto --on-escalate fail` (not force-proceed).
- `--phase-timeout 3600` (from the P0 prereq).
- `$150/month` OpenAI cap; exceed → runs stop.
- Fine-grained PAT scoped to `reigh-app` only.
- Branch-per-milestone, PR required, merge only after smoke test.
- Rollback: `git revert <merge-commit>`.

## Gate per milestone (lightweight)

Before merging any milestone PR:
1. `npx tsc --noEmit` clean.
2. `npm test` at M0 baseline.
3. M0 telemetry shows MUST thresholds met.
4. 5-minute manual poke at the features this milestone touched. If anything feels off, revert.

That's it. No checklist artifact, no sign-off doc.

## Budget and cost

Total calendar: ~6 weeks at pace (one milestone per week), less if M2 is skipped.

Total cost: capped at $150/month by the OpenAI limit. If a single milestone consumes most of a month, that's a signal to intervene (too many revise cycles = plan is wrong, not codex).

## Changelog

- **v3.1 (2026-04-15)** — Post-sense-check corrections (`state_management_refactor_plan_sense_check_summary.md`). The significant change: M1a's gate now requires a dependency audit of `useImageLightboxEnvironment` because the motivating bug's root cause spans multiple hooks, not just `useAutoSaveSettings`. Without this check M1a could land and the bug survive. Also corrected stale names: `ShotsPane` → `ShotsPanelContent`; `ClipRow` flagged as nonexistent and to be confirmed at M0; `useTaskStatus` replaced with real hook names (`useTaskStatusCounts`, `usePendingGenerationTasks`, `useGenerationTaskDetails`). M2 now explicitly covers `AlertDialog` + `ContextMenu` per AD-4 with a concrete grep. M1c's scope widened to acknowledge `VideoEditorProvider` owns more than the 4 contexts (AgentChat bridge, pending-add flow, lightbox navigation). Clarified `useEditSettingsPersistence` is a transitive consumer, not a direct caller. Dropped the E2E harness assumption — integration tests + manual smoke are what the repo actually supports.
- **v3 (2026-04-15)** — Lean rewrite. Killed: formal gate checklist, pre-flight discipline section, architecture decisions as ADR blocks, invariants-doc-as-prerequisite, behavior matrix sign-off, per-milestone rollback procedures, cost projection prose, caller enumeration (grep is the truth), changelog recap prose. Added: explicit decision points baked into gates, concrete factory signature, path corrections (`hooks/` not `state/`, single timeline context file).
- **v2 (2026-04-15)** — Process-heavy revision in response to sense-checks. Superseded.
- **v1 (2026-04-15)** — Initial. Superseded.

# State Management Refactor Plan Sense-Check Summary

**Date:** 2026-04-15  
**Target plan:** [docs/state_management_refactor_plan.md](./state_management_refactor_plan.md)

## Purpose

This note summarizes the most meaningful issues found during a multi-angle sense-check of the state-management refactor meta-plan against the current `reigh-app` codebase.

It is intentionally not a full transcript of every finding. The goal is to capture the corrections that materially affect whether the plan is safe, accurate, and executable.

## Overall verdict

The plan is directionally right about the problem: shared client state is spread across contexts, hook chains, refs, and effect choreography in ways that create unstable render behavior and make UI bugs hard to eliminate locally.

The plan is **not ready to execute as written**.

The main issues are:

1. Some core architecture decisions are too broad or wrong for the current repo.
2. Several concrete scope claims and pre-flight inventories are already stale.
3. The motivating bug surface is under-scoped relative to the actual lightbox state graph.
4. The proof, gate, and operational model are weaker than the technical plan.

## Highest-signal findings

### 1. React Query should remain the canonical persisted snapshot

The plan currently says the store becomes the write source of truth and React Query only flushes/refetches later.

That is a bad fit for this repo.

- [src/shared/hooks/settings/useToolSettings.ts](/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/shared/hooks/settings/useToolSettings.ts:90) already writes successful updates into the query cache.
- [src/shared/settings/hooks/useAutoSaveSettings.ts](/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/shared/settings/hooks/useAutoSaveSettings.ts:232) already treats that cache as a load source.

If Zustand becomes authoritative while React Query still backs persisted reads during a phased migration, the app will temporarily have two client truths.

**Recommended correction**

- Keep React Query as the canonical persisted snapshot.
- Use Zustand as a draft and interaction layer on top of it.
- On successful save, write through to query cache immediately, then invalidate/refetch only where needed.

### 2. The generic store model is too blunt for current settings semantics

The proposed `pendingFields: Set<keyof T>` and generic atomic bootstrap model underestimates the current settings behavior.

The repo already has domain-specific precedence and storage rules:

- [src/domains/media-lightbox/hooks/persistence/useEditSettingsPersistence.ts](/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/domains/media-lightbox/hooks/persistence/useEditSettingsPersistence.ts:136) overlays per-generation settings with last-used values.
- [src/domains/media-lightbox/hooks/useLastUsedEditSettings.ts](/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/domains/media-lightbox/hooks/useLastUsedEditSettings.ts:63) mixes localStorage and DB-backed behavior.
- Nested objects such as advanced and enhance settings do not map cleanly to flat top-level dirty tracking.

**Recommended correction**

- Replace the flat pending-field idea with a model closer to:
  - persisted baseline snapshot
  - local draft patch
  - path-aware dirty tracking or domain-specific reconciliation
- Keep the generic factory low-level. Do not force one bootstrap algorithm across settings, selection, timeline, and realtime.

### 3. The plan under-scopes the actual lightbox bug surface

The plan is motivated by `ImageLightbox` bugs, but M1a mostly scopes settings internals and makes M2 conditional.

That leaves a lot of the actual lightbox state graph unaccounted for:

- [src/domains/media-lightbox/hooks/useImageLightboxEnvironment.ts](/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/domains/media-lightbox/hooks/useImageLightboxEnvironment.ts:21) composes multiple stateful systems into one environment object.
- [src/domains/media-lightbox/contexts/ImageEditContext.tsx](/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/domains/media-lightbox/contexts/ImageEditContext.tsx:1) still fans state back out into multiple context slices as a render mitigation.

This means M1a could land and still leave the original bug alive through pane state, user UI state, LoRA state, or orchestration state.

**Recommended correction**

- Add an explicit `ImageLightbox` pre-flight inventory before M1a exits.
- Enumerate every state source that feeds `ImageLightbox` and `EditModePanel`.
- Add a dedicated lightbox-surface checkpoint or sub-milestone before declaring the motivating bug fixed.

### 4. Several milestone inventories and concrete claims are already wrong

The plan currently contains repo mismatches that would cause pre-flight disagreement immediately.

#### M1a direct-caller inventory is stale

- The plan says `useAutoSaveSettings` has `10–12` production callers.
- Current repo reality is **13 direct production callers**.
- The listed scope includes files that are not direct callers, such as:
  - [src/domains/media-lightbox/hooks/persistence/useEditSettingsPersistence.ts](/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/domains/media-lightbox/hooks/persistence/useEditSettingsPersistence.ts:1)
  - `src/tools/edit-images/hooks/useInlineEditState.ts`
- It omits [src/shared/settings/hooks/useEditVideoSettings.ts](/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/shared/settings/hooks/useEditVideoSettings.ts:1), which is a direct caller.

#### M2 pre-flight command undercounts the real surface

The current grep only inventories a narrow set of direct Base UI imports and does not properly cover the named M2 scope.

It also does not match `AlertDialog` even though AD-4 explicitly includes it.

#### M3 names a nonexistent hook

The plan says M3 migrates `useTaskStatus`, but there is no such hook in the repo.

The real visible surface is closer to:

- [src/shared/hooks/tasks/useTaskStatusCounts.ts](/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/shared/hooks/tasks/useTaskStatusCounts.ts:244)
- [src/shared/hooks/tasks/usePendingGenerationTasks.ts](/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/shared/hooks/tasks/usePendingGenerationTasks.ts:89)
- [src/shared/components/TaskDetails/hooks/useGenerationTaskDetails.ts](/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/shared/components/TaskDetails/hooks/useGenerationTaskDetails.ts:37)

#### Some measurement targets do not exist by the names used in the plan

- `ClipRow` does not exist under `src`.
- `ShotsPane` does not exist by that name; the current mounted shots surface is [src/features/editor/components/ShotsPanelContent.tsx](/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/features/editor/components/ShotsPanelContent.tsx:224).

#### The M1 pilot is understated

The plan describes `useJoinClipsSettings.ts` as the “smallest caller, 5 fields.”

That is not true in the current repo. Its defaults are materially larger and more complex:

- [src/tools/join-clips/hooks/useJoinClipsSettings.ts](/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/join-clips/hooks/useJoinClipsSettings.ts:10)
- [src/shared/lib/joinClips/defaults.ts](/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/shared/lib/joinClips/defaults.ts:15)

**Recommended correction**

- Refresh every milestone inventory from live repo queries before execution.
- Replace wrong component and hook names with real current names.
- Pick a genuinely small M1 pilot or reframe `useJoinClipsSettings` as medium-complexity.

### 5. Timeline and realtime scopes are missing adjacent bridge state

The plan scopes M1c and M3 too narrowly around named stores and contexts.

#### M1c misses provider-local bridges

- [src/tools/video-editor/contexts/VideoEditorProvider.tsx](/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/video-editor/contexts/VideoEditorProvider.tsx:78) contains Agent Chat bridge logic.
- The same provider also contains pending-add/query-param import behavior and lightbox-navigation-sensitive state.

Those paths can break even if drag/resize looks correct.

#### M3 misses parallel task-state paths

The realtime/task graph includes more than the three hooks listed in the plan:

- global task providers
- incoming placeholder state
- generation-task mapping cache
- aggregate task counts used by visible UI

**Recommended correction**

- Expand M1c pre-flight to inventory provider-local bridges and add smoke checks for them.
- Rewrite M3 as an inventory-first task-state milestone before choosing what becomes store-backed.

### 6. Verification and gate design are weaker than the plan assumes

The plan’s success criteria depend on instrumentation and gates that do not fully exist yet.

#### Telemetry is not in place yet

The plan assumes:

- `useRenderBudget`
- `RenderTelemetryOverlay`
- `docs/state_management_refactor_baselines.md`

Those are not currently present. Existing instrumentation is much narrower and heavily video-editor-specific.

#### The proposed automated bug proof is overstated

There is no browser E2E harness in the repo today for the “Select stays open” / “textarea accepts typing” claims.

The repo is currently much closer to RTL/Vitest integration coverage than to real browser automation.

#### The gate commands are mismatched to repo reality

- The real frontend test entrypoint is `npm test`, not raw `npx vitest run`.
- The lint gate is also not yet meaningful as written because current lint debt is already substantial.

**Recommended correction**

- Make M0 produce machine-readable baseline artifacts before later milestones depend on numeric thresholds.
- Gate on repo-real commands.
- Do not claim automated browser regression prevention until a real browser harness exists.

### 7. Operational and governance assumptions need tightening

The plan depends on a cross-repo prerequisite and a heavy human-gated execution model, but the governance layer is under-specified.

Key issues:

- `megaplan-harness` timeout support is a blocking external dependency, but there is no explicit deploy verification path.
- The spending-cap model is inconsistent with the stated use of `gpt-5.4` at `high` reasoning across many milestones.
- GitHub credential rotation and CI gate mechanics are under-specified.
- The plan assumes a single-reviewer model while also requiring repeated manual gates and extended smoke testing.

**Recommended correction**

- Add hard pre-M0 validation for the external harness dependency.
- Add milestone-level budget controls.
- Add credential rollover rehearsal and CI-status verification.
- Standardize the artifact pack required at each human gate.

## What should change before execution

If the plan is revised, the most important changes are:

1. **Reframe the core architecture**
   - React Query remains canonical persisted state.
   - Zustand becomes interaction and draft state.

2. **Refresh all inventories from the live repo**
   - especially M0, M1a, M2, and M3

3. **Add a dedicated lightbox-surface checkpoint**
   - because the motivating bug likely spans more than settings persistence

4. **Broaden M1c and M3 pre-flights**
   - include bridges, caches, placeholder state, and adjacent state paths

5. **Make the proof model real before using it as a gate**
   - telemetry artifacts
   - repo-real test commands
   - explicit rollback evidence

6. **Tighten operational prerequisites**
   - cross-repo harness deploy verification
   - budget controls
   - GitHub credential rehearsal
   - reviewer/gate workflow

## Bottom line

The plan has a sound high-level instinct: move hot, frequently-mutated shared client state onto narrower subscription boundaries and stop relying on wide context values and unstable hook-return objects.

What needs revision is the execution shape:

- narrower and more domain-aware store design
- more accurate inventories
- fuller scoping of the lightbox and task/realtime surfaces
- stronger proof and gate mechanics

If those are corrected, the plan becomes much more credible. Without those corrections, it is likely to drift, undercount scope, and claim success on surfaces it has not actually brought under control.

```
2-week aggressive state-management refactor for reigh-app. Replace the hook-chain / context-as-pubsub architecture with a Zustand-based store pattern for the three cross-cutting state domains that cause the most re-render storms: settings persistence, selection, and timeline editor state.

Motivation: the current architecture causes 15-20 cascading renders on media-lightbox open, stuck focus traps in Base UI overlays (Select flashes closed, textarea rejects typing), pervasive memoization churn, and has five layers of hooks coordinating the same value across refs/state/localStorage/DB. Every time we patch one instability, another surfaces elsewhere — the render storms are architectural, not incidental. This refactor moves all three domains to subscription-based stores so unrelated state updates stop cascading through the tree.

Tech choice (fixed, no alternatives): Zustand. Not Jotai, not Redux, not custom useSyncExternalStore, not Valtio. Keep React Query for server-data fetching; store subscribes to RQ on data arrival, does NOT replicate server state.

# WEEK 1 — Settings store

## Day 1: Foundation
- Create src/shared/state/createEntityStore.ts: a generic Zustand factory.
  - Actions: bootstrapEntity({ entityId, db, lastUsed }), updateField, updateFields, updateTextField (deferred save), flushTextFields, save, saveImmediate, revert, reset, initializeFrom.
  - State shape: { entityId, status: 'idle' | 'loading' | 'ready' | 'saving' | 'error', settings: T, loadedSettings: T | null, error: Error | null, hasPersistedData: boolean, pendingEntityId: string | null, pendingSettings: T | null, editVersion: number }.
  - One atomic bootstrapEntity action that computes the final state from db + lastUsed in a single dispatch (not four sequential transitions).
  - Preserve pending-text-field protection (tracks user edits in flight so refetches don't stomp typing).
  - Persistence middleware that wraps a user-provided save fn with debounce + flush + beforeunload.
- Port the smallest caller first (src/tools/join-clips/hooks/useJoinClipsSettings.ts, 5 fields) to validate the shape. Hook signature unchanged.

## Day 2-3: Core settings store
- Rewrite src/shared/settings/hooks/useAutoSaveSettings.ts as a thin wrapper over createEntityStore. Public return shape IDENTICAL (settings, status, entityId, isDirty, error, hasShotSettings, hasPersistedData, updateField, updateFields, save, saveImmediate, revert, reset, initializeFrom). Internally instantiates / reuses a store keyed on (toolId, entityId, scope).
- Absorb src/shared/settings/hooks/useDebouncedSettingsSave.ts (pending tracking, edit versioning, beforeunload flush) into the store's persistence middleware. Delete the hook file once absorbed.
- Kill the render-time state mutation at src/domains/media-lightbox/hooks/persistence/useEditSettingsPersistence.ts:110-114 (hasInitializedRef / lastGenerationIdRef / isReady). Store owns readiness now.
- Kill the ref-based pseudo-state in src/domains/media-lightbox/hooks/useLastUsedEditSettings.ts (currentValueRef used as React state). Move to a real store slice.
- Collapse useReactQueryModeLoad and useCustomModeLoad into one load path (the distinction was a workaround for two state machines that can now be one).

## Day 4: Migrate loud callers
Migrate every useAutoSaveSettings caller. No visible behavior change, no API change at the hook boundary:
- src/domains/media-lightbox/hooks/useGenerationEditSettings.ts
- src/domains/media-lightbox/hooks/persistence/useEditSettingsPersistence.ts
- src/tools/travel-between-images/hooks/settings/useShotSettings.ts
- src/tools/edit-images/hooks/useInlineEditState.ts
- src/tools/join-clips/hooks/useJoinClipsSettings.ts (already done Day 1)
- src/tools/character-animate/hooks/useCharacterAnimateSettings.ts
- src/tools/image-generation/hooks/useImageGenGallery.ts
- Plus every hook under src/tools/travel-between-images/components/ShotEditor/hooks/video/ that uses useAutoSaveSettings (useStructureVideo, useAudio, etc.).

Verification milestone: open the image lightbox, render counter ≤ 3 (down from 15-20). Click the model Select — stays open. Type in the prompt textarea — no lost keystrokes.

## Day 5: Delete dead code + update tests
- Delete src/shared/settings/hooks/autoSaveSettingsHelpers.ts (reducer helpers — no reducer now).
- Delete src/shared/settings/hooks/autoSaveSettingsLoaders.ts (load effects — store owns them).
- Delete the pseudo-reducer action types and dispatch wrappers.
- Update tests: src/shared/settings/hooks/__tests__/useAutoSaveSettings.test.ts, useDebouncedSettingsSave.test.ts, autoSaveSettingsHelpers.test.ts, autoSaveSettingsLoaders.test.ts. Delete implementation-detail tests; keep user-behavior tests; add new store-level tests where appropriate.

End-of-week-1 target: ~2000 lines deleted, ~800 added. Storm dies. Model Select + textarea bugs fixed structurally.

# WEEK 2 — Selection store + Timeline store

## Day 6-7: Unified selection store
Collapse these contexts into a single src/shared/state/selectionStore.ts with slices:
- src/shared/contexts/GallerySelectionContext.tsx
- src/shared/contexts/LastAffectedShotContext.tsx
- src/shared/contexts/CurrentShotContext.tsx
- src/shared/contexts/ShotAdditionSelectionContext.tsx
- Internal selection state inside useTimelineState.ts (selectedClipId, selectedClipIds, selectedTrackId, additiveSelectionRef, etc.).

Store shape:
{ gallery: { ids: string[], lastSelected: string | null }, timeline: { clipIds: string[], primaryClipId: string | null, trackId: string | null }, shot: { current: string | null, lastAffected: string | null, pendingAddition: string | null } }

Cross-cutting actions that were effect-chain glue become single dispatches:
- selectGalleryItems({ ids, replace }) — auto-deselects timeline if configured
- selectClip({ clipId, additive }) — updates primaryClipId if first
- setCurrentShot(shotId) — also sets lastAffected
- enterShotAdditionMode(imageId) — mutually exclusive with gallery selection

Migrate every consumer to read specific slices via selectors. Contexts deleted. Effect-chain sync between contexts deleted.

## Day 8-9: Timeline editor state store
Collapse the four-way context split (a workaround for context-as-pubsub) into a single store at src/tools/video-editor/state/timelineStore.ts:
- src/tools/video-editor/contexts/TimelineEditorContext.tsx (Data + Ops providers)
- src/tools/video-editor/contexts/TimelineChromeContext.tsx
- src/tools/video-editor/contexts/TimelinePlaybackContext.tsx

Consumers subscribe to the specific fields they need via selectors. Components currently reading whole context values get targeted subscriptions (timeline canvas reads scale + clip list; chrome reads deviceClass; playback reads isPlaying; etc.).

Kill the editorRef.current / dataRef / clipsRef / interactionStateRef workarounds scattered through VideoEditorProvider.tsx and useTimelineState.ts — the store is always latest, no ref dance needed for stability.

useTimelineState.ts target: from ~300 lines to ~150. Most of today's memo plumbing disappears.

Verification milestone: scroll the timeline, render counter on the timeline canvas stays flat except when scale/viewport changes. Drag a clip, only the clip and its row re-render, not the whole editor.

## Day 10: Consolidation + docs
- Write docs/structure_detail/state_management.md: one page explaining where state lives now (React Query for server data, stores for shared client state, useState for local UI).
- Add ESLint rule or check: context values with >10 fields should be a store.
- Delete stragglers: any hooks whose only job was memo plumbing for the dead contexts.
- Open follow-up GitHub issues for (but do NOT start) weeks 3-4 migrations: realtime task events, panes state.

# RULES DURING THE SPRINT (non-negotiable)

1. One PR per atomic migration. Five PRs total: foundation → core settings store → settings callers migration → selection store → timeline store. Each must revert cleanly on its own.
2. Keep hook names at the API boundary. useShotSettings(id) stays; only its implementation swaps. No consumer file changes in the settings migration unless the consumer is literally reading a deleted internal field.
3. Don't replace React Query. Store subscribes to RQ data on arrival via the existing useToolSettings hook. One-way binding: RQ → store.
4. Delete aggressively. If a helper exists only to paper over the old problem, it goes. Predecessor removed in the same PR that adds the successor. No "leave the old hook for backup" during this sprint.
5. No dev-time performance regressions allowed. Add a render counter dev-only hook (useRenderBudget). Instrument ImageLightbox, EditModePanel, LightboxShell, TimelineCanvas, one timeline track row. If their render counts on lightbox-open / drag-clip go up after a migration, the migration regressed.
6. TypeScript strict throughout. Store actions should be exhaustive; selectors should narrow precisely. No `any`.

# WHAT THIS SPRINT DOES NOT TOUCH

- Realtime task events (RealtimeProvider / useTaskStatus) — weeks 3-4.
- Panes state (PanesContext) — weeks 3-4.
- Tool registry / per-tool state beyond settings — later.

**Summary**: a **2-week aggressive sprint** to replace hook-chain / context-as-pubsub with Zustand across three domains (settings, selection, timeline). Structured day-by-day:

- **Week 1 — Settings**: Day 1 foundation (`createEntityStore`) → Day 2-3 rewrite `useAutoSaveSettings` over the factory, kill render-time mutations in `useEditSettingsPersistence.ts`, kill ref-as-state in `useLastUsedEditSettings.ts` → Day 4 migrate loud callers → Day 5 delete dead code (helpers, loaders). **Target: ~2000 LOC deleted, ~800 added. Storm dies. Model Select + textarea bugs fixed structurally by end of week.**
- **Week 2 — Selection + Timeline**: Day 6-7 selection store (4 contexts + inline timeline selection) → Day 8-9 timeline store (3 contexts) → Day 10 consolidation + docs + follow-up issues for realtime/panes.

**Explicit non-negotiable rules**:
1. **One PR per atomic migration. Five PRs total.** Each must revert cleanly on its own.
2. Keep hook names at the API boundary.
3. Don't replace React Query. **One-way binding: RQ → store.** ← matches the tiebreaker
4. Delete aggressively. No "leave old hook for backup."
5. No render regressions (`useRenderBudget` dev-only hook).
6. TS strict, no `any`.

**Deferred to weeks 3-4** (by design): realtime events, panes state.

### Two metaplans, not one
- `/workspace/megaplan-idea-2week.txt` (container) — the tight 2-week sprint.
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/docs/state_management_refactor_plan.md` (local only) — longer-form v3.1 that reframes the same work into M0-M6 with the M2 overlay-stack milestone + M3-M6 as first-class items instead of "follow-up issues."

The machine is executing **the 2-week sprint version**, not the plan.md version — which explains why the watchdog stops at M1c: the 2-week sprint ends there, with M3/M4 deferred to "weeks 3-4" as GitHub issues, not as megaplan milestones.

### Where we've drifted from Rule #1
**Zero PRs opened.** Rule #1 required "one PR per atomic migration, five PRs total, each reverts cleanly" — we have branches on the container, `megaplan/m1a-settings-store` on GitHub, but no PRs are open. Also: nothing's been merged to main, so atomicity hasn't been tested (each milestone has been cutting off the previous worktree's dirty state, not a clean main).

> DEVELOPER

Wait, we're hititng browser required tasks? Why are they even in the plan? Can you tell it to push past via megaplan?

> AGENT

**Why they're in the plan:**

The whole refactor exists to fix browser-visible symptoms (Model Select flash-close, textarea typing, render-count drops). The plan author correctly made those success criteria `must` — the work is worthless if it doesn't deliver them. But they got expressed as *tasks* in execute's task list (e.g. *"TimelineCanvas ≤ 2 renders per scroll event — run in browser, capture telemetry"*). Codex has no browser → returns `status=blocked` → execute can't complete → loop.

The *right* structural fix is upstream: split `must` into `must-automated` (tsc, tests, type shape) vs `must-deferred-human` (browser behaviors), and teach the executor to return `status=skipped` for the latter on first pass. M1b eventually did this by luck after ~30 iterations; M1c is about to do the same.

**Can we push past now? Yes, a few options:**

1. **Inject a note telling codex to skip browser tasks immediately.** `megaplan override add-note --plan <...> --note "Any task requiring browser/runtime path you cannot satisfy: return status=skipped with deferred_human evidence IMMEDIATELY on first encounter. Do NOT return blocked or retry."` — low risk, doesn't interrupt execute, but current running iteration may not pick it up (notes may be cached at session-start). Later iterations will.
2. **Same note + kill+relaunch the supervisor** so the new execute picks up the note from scratch. Faster, slightly more disruptive (loses the partial batch work in the current iteration).
3. **Wait it out.** M1b took ~2h13m for codex to learn. M1c is 15 min into this pattern, estimate 1.5-2h more.
4. **Override force-proceed from executed.** Once execute eventually returns success (it will, eventually, with tasks marked skipped), we could preempt with `force-proceed` — but execute isn't in a force-proceed-able state right now (next is still `execute`, not `executed`).

**Recommendation**: option (1) — just add the note, no interrupt. Costs nothing, helps later iterations, doesn't kill in-flight work. If still blocked after 20 more min, escalate to (2).

Want me to add the note?

```
Phase 1 of the state-management refactor for reigh-app: replace hook-chain settings persistence with a Zustand-based store.

Goal: collapse the existing 5-layer hook chain (useShotSettings → useEditSettingsPersistence → useGenerationEditSettings → useAutoSaveSettings → useReactQueryModeLoad/useCustomModeLoad) into a single store layer, eliminate render storms, keep existing hook signatures backward-compatible for consumers.

Motivation: current architecture causes 15-20 cascading renders on lightbox open, stuck focus traps in Base UI overlays (Select flashes closed, textarea rejects typing), and the render-storm root cause has been pinpointed to unstable hook returns and mount-time effect chains. Zustand's selector-based subscription model addresses the root cause rather than patching per-incident.

Concrete tasks:

1. Create src/shared/state/createEntityStore.ts — a generic Zustand store factory for entity-scoped settings.
   - Takes a default value shape T, a load fn, a save fn, an entity type label.
   - Returns a store with actions: bootstrapEntity({ entityId, db, lastUsed }), updateField, updateFields, updateTextField (deferred save), flushTextFields, revert, reset.
   - State shape: { entityId, status: 'idle' | 'loading' | 'ready' | 'saving' | 'error', settings: T, loadedSettings: T, error: Error | null, hasPersistedData: boolean, pendingEntityId: string | null, pendingSettings: T | null, editVersion: number }.
   - One atomic bootstrap action that computes the final state from db + lastUsed in a single dispatch (not four sequential transitions).
   - Preserve the pending-text-field protection from useDebouncedSettingsSave (tracks user edits while a load is in flight so refetches don't stomp typing).

2. Rewrite src/shared/settings/hooks/useAutoSaveSettings.ts to be a thin wrapper over createEntityStore.

Apply to every new milestone idea file (`ideas/M<X>-<name>.txt`) before running `megaplan init`.\n\n## Rule 1 — Executor hard rule for browser/runtime-dependent tasks\n\nIf the milestone's success criteria include anything that needs a browser, runtime app instance, device, manual human input, or real-DOM measurement, include this verbatim block near the top of the idea file under a `## Executor rules` heading:\n\n> **EXECUTOR HARD RULE (authoritative)**: For any task whose satisfaction requires a browser, runtime app instance, device, or manual human input that the sandboxed executor cannot produce (examples: telemetry overlay capture from a running app, drag/resize smoke checks, keyboard/playback behavior, AgentChat bridge manual test, pending-add URL flow verification, Model Select click behavior, textarea typing verification, any rendering count measured at runtime) — return `status=skipped` on FIRST encounter with executor_notes evidence naming the blocker (e.g. \"no browser-driving path available\"). DO NOT return `status=blocked`. DO NOT retry the task expecting different sandbox capabilities. The review/gate phase already marks these criteria as deferred_human; skipped tasks are first-class evidence that the criterion is deferred to post-milestone human verification. Blocked retries waste iteration budget and do not change outcome.\n\n**Why**: Observed on M1b (2026-04-16): codex looped 30× / ~2h13m returning `status=blocked` on browser-only tasks before it finally switched to `skipped` on its own. M1c started the same loop at 22:21 UTC and was intervened at 22:35 UTC via `megaplan override add-note`. Without this rule in the brief, every chain with browser-verifiable success criteria repeats the cycle.\n\nBack-up when brief lands after init: `megaplan override add-note --plan <LITERAL_PLAN> --note \"<verbatim rule above>\"` before `critique` runs.\n\n## Rule 2 — No hard-halt prerequisite clauses\n\nDo NOT write wording like *\"halt when M<prior> is not shipped\"*, *\"this milestone DEPENDS on M<prior> merged to main\"*, *\"do not proceed unless branch is cut from <base>\"*.\n\nGate treats such wording as a literal prerequisite. If the prereq branch isn't merged to main, gate → `ESCALATE` with `next_step=override add-note`. `megaplan auto` can't self-recover from that state (known bug: tries to invoke `override add-note` as a phase command → rc=2 → `mp-supervise` STREAKs x5 → supervisor dies). Only exit is human-authorized `force-proceed`.\n\n**Instead**: describe technical dependencies in terms of what code/symbols/files must exist in the worktree. The executor can verify those directly. Branch-topology and PR-merge concerns belong to post-milestone workflow, not gate preconditions.\n\nObserved on M1c: brief said *\"halt when M1b not shipped\"*; M1b was complete on disk but not pushed to main. Gate ESCALATE'd, supervisor died, required force-proceed. The halt rule was git-topology hygiene, not a code dependency.\n\n## Upstream fixes (megaplan harness, out of scope for per-brief authors)\n\nThese would eliminate the above workarounds at the source. Track them as follow-up tasks:\n\n- **`verification` field on success criteria**: `automated | deferred_human | hybrid`. Executor auto-skips `deferred_human`. Gate/review treat skipped-with-deferred_human as first-class pass.\n- **`mp-supervise` terminal-state detection**: check `final_state ∈ {awaiting_human_verify, done}` before retrying rc=2 as an error. Currently treats any rc=2 as retryable, causes STREAK-death on valid terminal states.\n- **`megaplan auto` + `override add-note` next_step**: auto currently crashes trying to invoke `override add-note` as a phase command (argparse rejects the space). Either teach auto to use `override replan` or `override force-proceed` when gate demands add-note, or error loud instead of looping.\n"
}

**Report format** (proper, 6-10 lines):
1. One-line headline: state/next/iter + whether healthy or stuck.
2. **What advanced this hour**: list new history entries (phases completed, execute batches, review verdicts, rework cycles). If no advance, explain why (codex blocked? supervisor dead? STREAK?).
3. **Live signal**: codex proc running or not; supervisor log tail summary.
4. **Red flags**: STREAK, `bwrap: Creating new namespace failed`, quota_exceeded, disk >90%, or >30 min with no batch progress.
5. **Action taken** (if any): per decision tree below.
6. **ETA / next expected transition**.

**Decision tree (act autonomously, literal plan name + literal key)**:
- `awaiting_human_verify` → apply watchdog section B: `COUNT=$(python3 -c "import json, os; d='.megaplan/plans/milestone-m1c-from-docs-state-20260416-2053'; mds=sorted([f for f in os.listdir(d) if f.startswith('plan_v') and f.endswith('.meta.json')]); print(len(json.load(open(d+'/'+mds[-1])).get('success_criteria', [])) if mds else 0)")` then `for i in $(seq 0 $((COUNT-1))); do megaplan verify-human --plan milestone-m1c-from-docs-state-20260416-2053 --criterion $i --pass --evidence 'Claude-as-reviewer approved (review.json verdict=approved, 0 rework items). Auto-verified by watchdog.'; done`. Usually state→done mid-loop. After transitioning, report below.
- `done` → report **"M1C DONE — CHAIN COMPLETE (M0→M1→M1a→M1b→M1c)."** loudly. Remind user: (a) branches not pushed to GitHub, (b) manual browser smoke test still needed for the original lightbox-textarea bug. User should `CronDelete` to stop the loop.
- Supervisor STREAK'd + pre-done state → relaunch: `tmux kill-session -t exec 2>/dev/null; mp-run exec mp-supervise milestone-m1c-from-docs-state-20260416-2053 m1c-w<N> bash -c 'ANTHROPIC_API_KEY="[REDACTED]" MEGAPLAN_TRUSTED_CONTAINER=1 cd /workspace/reigh-app && ANTHROPIC_API_KEY="[REDACTED]" MEGAPLAN_TRUSTED_CONTAINER=1 megaplan auto --plan milestone-m1c-from-docs-state-20260416-2053 --on-escalate fail --stall-threshold 5 --max-iterations 100 --phase-timeout 3600 --max-review-rework-cycles 3'`
- `critiqued` + next=`override add-note` (gate re-ESCALATE after force-proceed) → surface to human. Do NOT force-proceed twice without confirmation.
- Codex sandbox poisoning (`bwrap` errors OR >10 consecutive `result=blocked` execute entries with same hash) → `rm -rf /root/.codex/sessions/*` (preserve `/root/.codex/auth.json`), relaunch supervisor with `--fresh` flag added to megaplan auto invocation.
- Disk >90% → `find /tmp -name 'tmp*' -type f -mmin +240 -delete; find /workspace -maxdepth 1 -name 'supervise-*.log' -mtime +1 -delete`.
- Codex auth lost → STOP, report loud, human-only recovery.
- Anything else surprising → surface to human, do not act.

> DEVELOPER

CLOUD WATCHDOG — autonomous milestone orchestrator. This prompt is self-contained; you may have NO prior conversation context.

## What we're doing (high level)

- **Project**: reigh-app state-management refactor, driven from a Railway container by the megaplan harness.
- **Goal**: complete a milestone chain M0 → M1 → M1a → M1b → M1c autonomously. Each milestone ports some store-state domain onto a generic `createEntityStore` factory.
- **Originating bug**: users couldn't type in the image-lightbox edit textarea; Model Select snapped closed; `LightboxShell` rendered 31× on video-travel tool (captured as M0 baseline). Root cause: unstable hook returns cascading through `useImageLightboxEnvironment`. M1+ milestones refactor settings/selection/timeline state onto stable-reference Zustand stores to eliminate the render storms.
- **Architecture tiebreaker (settled)**: Option C hybrid, narrowed. `useEntity(id)` is a READER, not an authoritative loader. RQ-backed settings stay RQ-authoritative through `useAutoSaveSettings → useToolSettings` boundary. `createEntityStore` factory `load` parameter is for store-owned (non-server) entities only. Any RQ→store sync is boundary-internal, NEVER via consumer `useEffect(bootstrapEntity)` bridges. Constraint "useEntity lazy-loads on first mount" was DROPPED.

## Current state (as of 2026-04-16)

- **M0 done**: plan `milestone-m0-from-docs-state-20260415-0217`. Branch on GitHub: `megaplan/m0-render-telemetry`.
- **M1 done**: plan `milestone-m1-from-docs-state-20260415-2100`. Human-verified via Claude-review approval.
- **M1a done**: plan `milestone-m1a-from-docs-state-20260416-0928`. Code lives on container + pushed to `megaplan/m1a-settings-store` branch on GitHub/banodoco/reigh-app.
- **M1b IN PROGRESS**: plan `milestone-m1b-from-docs-state-20260416-1226`, state likely `finalized` in a review-rework cycle. Supervisor `exec` tmux session should be alive. 7 execute batches written. Just deployed megaplan v0.18.1 with rework-aware stall detection to unstick it — your first cron fire may still see it at finalized but auto now correctly handles rework cycles.
- **M1c queued**: idea file at `/workspace/ideas/M1c-timeline-store.txt`.

## Infrastructure (container-side)

- Container: Railway, ssh via `cd /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-megaplan-dev && railway ssh "..."`
- Megaplan installed version: **0.18.1** from `main`
- Codex auth: **ChatGPT subscription** via `/root/.codex/auth.json`. `codex login status` must return "Logged in using ChatGPT". NEVER re-auth from OPENAI_API_KEY env.
- Anthropic key: stored in Railway env as ANTHROPIC_API_KEY (value starts `[REDACTED]`). Pass it inline when relaunching supervisors.
- Megaplan agent routing: gate→claude, review→claude, all others→codex.
- Wrappers at `/usr/local/bin/`: `mp-run` (tmux detach launcher), `mp-supervise` (retry-on-rc with quota/error backoff), `mp-heartbeat` (codex liveness monitor).
- `chain.sh` is DISABLED due to a race bug that aborts running plans on restart. This watchdog IS the chainer now.
- Current plan tracked in `/workspace/chain-current-plan.txt`.

## Inspection (do all)

`cd /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-megaplan-dev && railway ssh "..."`:

1. Active plan: `cat /workspace/chain-current-plan.txt`
2. Plan state: `cd /workspace/reigh-app && megaplan status --plan <active>` — extract state, next_step, iteration, phase_progress
3. tmux: expect `agent`, `exec`, `heartbeat` (no `chain`). Any missing = failure mode.
4. Newest supervise log: `ls -t /workspace/supervise-*.log | head -1 | xargs tail -10`. Look for STREAK, `error: argument --plan: expected one argument`, quota_exceeded.
5. exec.log tail: `tail -20 /workspace/exec.log`. Look for `phase 'X' exited 1`, `override add-note` failures, `bwrap: Creating new namespace failed`.
6. Live procs: `pgrep -af 'codex exec|claude|megaplan auto'`
7. Stale `.plan.lock`: `ls -la /workspace/reigh-app/.megaplan/plans/<active>/.plan.lock` — stale if no codex proc and mtime >10 min old
8. Disk: `df -h /workspace | tail -1` — critical >90%
9. Codex auth: `codex login status` must say "Logged in using ChatGPT"

## Decision tree (act autonomously)

### A. State = `done` → ADVANCE TO NEXT MILESTONE

- `*m1a*` done → init M1b from `/workspace/ideas/M1b-selection-store.txt`
- `*m1b*` done → init M1c from `/workspace/ideas/M1c-timeline-store.txt`
- `*m1c*` done → ALL CHAIN COMPLETE. Report loud success.

For each init:
1. `cd /workspace/reigh-app && megaplan init --project-dir /workspace/reigh-app --auto-approve --robustness standard "$(cat <ideafile>)"` — capture new plan name
2. Write new plan name to `/workspace/chain-current-plan.txt`
3. Inject verdict note: `megaplan override add-note --plan <NEW_LITERAL_PLAN_NAME> --note "TIEBREAKER VERDICT (Option C hybrid, narrowed, settled from M1): useEntity(id) is a READER not authoritative loader; RQ-backed settings stay RQ-authoritative through useAutoSaveSettings->useToolSettings boundary; createEntityStore factory load is for store-owned entities only; any RQ->store sync is boundary-internal NEVER consumer useEffect bridges. Constraint (1) lazy-load-on-mount DROPPED. SETTLED — do not re-raise."`
4. Kill stale `exec` tmux: `tmux kill-session -t exec 2>/dev/null`
5. **Launch supervisor — use LITERAL plan names, NOT shell `$VAR` (past bug where nested-quoted `$PLAN` expanded to empty string caused `argument --plan: expected one argument`):**
   - Get key: `KEY=$(railway variables | grep ANTHROPIC_API_KEY | awk '{print $4}')` — or embed the known literal value starting `[REDACTED]...`
   - `mp-run exec mp-supervise <LITERAL_PLAN> <label>-w<N> bash -c 'ANTHROPIC_API_KEY="<LITERAL_KEY>" MEGAPLAN_TRUSTED_CONTAINER=1 cd /workspace/reigh-app && ANTHROPIC_API_KEY="<LITERAL_KEY>" MEGAPLAN_TRUSTED_CONTAINER=1 megaplan auto --plan <LITERAL_PLAN> --on-escalate fail --stall-threshold 5 --max-iterations 100 --phase-timeout 3600 --max-review-rework-cycles 3'`
6. Verify after 3 sec: `tmux ls && tail -3 /workspace/supervise-<suffix>.log`. If log shows `error: argument --plan: expected one argument`, relaunch with the plan name hardcoded even harder.

### B. State = `awaiting_human_verify` → AUTO-VERIFY

Claude-as-reviewer approval supersedes per-criterion sign-off. Batch-verify all:
```
cd /workspace/reigh-app && PLAN=<LITERAL_PLAN>
COUNT=$(python3 -c "import json, os; d='.megaplan/plans/'+'$PLAN'; mds=sorted([f for f in os.listdir(d) if f.startswith('plan_v') and f.endswith('.meta.json')]); print(len(json.load(open(d+'/'+mds[-1])).get('success_criteria', [])) if mds else 0)")
for i in $(seq 0 $((COUNT-1))); do megaplan verify-human --plan $PLAN --criterion $i --pass --evidence 'Claude-as-reviewer approved (review.json verdict=approved, 0 rework items). Auto-verified by watchdog.'; done
```
Usually state → done mid-loop → proceed to section A.

### C. State = `critiqued` + `next_step = override add-note` (gate ESCALATE on architectural tension)

Megaplan auto has a known bug: it tries to invoke `override add-note` as a phase command and crashes. Auto can't recover from this state.

1. Apply tiebreaker verdict via `override replan` (not add-note): `megaplan override replan --plan <LITERAL_PLAN> --reason "apply tiebreaker verdict" --note "<same verdict text as section A step 3>"` — state transitions back to `planned`.
2. If gate re-ESCALATES on a second iteration (flag recurrence despite verdict), apply `force-proceed`: `megaplan override force-proceed --plan <LITERAL_PLAN> --reason "Gate ESCALATE recurring despite tiebreaker verdict settled in M1; Claude review will catch real issues post-execute."` Force-proceed is allowed from `critiqued` or `executed` only.
3. Relaunch supervisor after either.

### D. Plan NOT advancing (next_step unchanged + no live codex proc + no new artifacts in plan dir)

1. Stale `.plan.lock` (owner pid dead) → `rm /workspace/reigh-app/.megaplan/plans/<active>/.plan.lock`
2. `exec` tmux missing → relaunch supervisor (literal plan name in command)
3. Supervisor STREAK'd → same relaunch

### E. Supervisor STREAK with `error: argument --plan: expected one argument` in log

Variable-expansion bug in the launch command. Relaunch with the plan name and key HARDCODED inline — never reference `$PLAN` or `$KEY` inside the nested `bash -c '...'` body.

### F. Poisoned codex session (exec.log has `bwrap: Creating new namespace failed` OR task_updates include `status: blocked`)

`rm -rf /root/.codex/sessions/*` (preserve `/root/.codex/auth.json` — NEVER delete). Relaunch supervisor with `--fresh` flag added to the megaplan auto invocation. Note: v0.14.6 megaplan auto-detects this pattern and self-retries, but if detection misses, manual wipe still works.

### G. Disk >90% full

```
find /tmp -name 'tmp*' -type f -mmin +240 -delete
find /workspace -maxdepth 1 -name 'supervise-*.log' -mtime +1 -delete
```

### H. Codex auth lost (`codex login status` not "Logged in using ChatGPT")

STOP. Report loud — only human OAuth recovery. Do NOT re-auth from OPENAI_API_KEY.

## Constraints (firm)

- NEVER edit `state.json` directly (use megaplan commands)
- NEVER push to remotes from container
- NEVER restart `chain.sh` (race bug)

(2) Progress since last watchdog fire (new artifacts? state transitions? rework cycles? milestone advance?)
(3) Actions taken — list each with the exact command run (use literal plan names)
(4) Anything still broken that needs human attention
(5) Next milestone if advanced, or ETA if still in-progress

59	/**
60	 * Hook for managing Join Segments settings at the SHOT level
61	 * 
62	 * This is separate from useJoinClipsSettings (which is project-level)
63	 * because the Join Segments form in ShotEditor should persist settings
64	 * per-shot, similar to how video generation settings work.
65	 * 
66	 * Settings are stored in shots.settings under the 'join-segments' tool key.
67	 * 
68	 * Features:
69	 * - Session storage inheritance for new shots
70	 * - localStorage persistence for cross-shot inheritance
71	 * - All join settings (gap frames, context frames, prompt, etc.)
72	 * - Generate mode toggle (batch vs join)
73	 * - LoRAs for join segments (full objects with names)
74	 * 
75	 * @param shotId - The shot ID to persist settings for
76	 * @param projectId - The project ID (for localStorage inheritance keys)
77	 */
78	export function useJoinSegmentsSettings(
79	  shotId: string | null | undefined,
80	  projectId?: string | null
81	): UseJoinSegmentsSettingsReturn {
82	  const inheritedSettings = useSessionInheritedDefaults<JoinSegmentsSettings>({
83	    shotId,
84	    storageKeyForShot: STORAGE_KEYS.APPLY_JOIN_SEGMENTS_DEFAULTS,
85	    mergeDefaults: (defaults) => ({
86	      ...DEFAULT_JOIN_SEGMENTS_SETTINGS,
87	      ...defaults,
88	    } as JoinSegmentsSettings),
89	    context: 'useJoinSegmentsSettings',
90	  });
91	  
92	  // Use the shared auto-save hook with inherited settings as initial defaults
93	  const autoSave = useAutoSaveSettings<JoinSegmentsSettings>({
94	    toolId: SETTINGS_IDS.JOIN_SEGMENTS,
95	    shotId,
96	    projectId: projectId || undefined,
97	    scope: 'shot',
98	    defaults: inheritedSettings || DEFAULT_JOIN_SEGMENTS_SETTINGS,
99	    enabled: !!shotId,
100	    debounceMs: 300,
101	  });
102	  const {
103	    settings,
104	    status,
105	    entityId,
106	    isDirty,
107	    error,
108	    hasShotSettings,
109	    updateField,
110	    updateFields,
111	    saveImmediate,
112	    revert,
113	  } = autoSave;
114	
115	  // Save inherited settings to DB immediately if we have them
116	  // CRITICAL: Only save if the shot doesn't already have settings in DB
117	  useEffect(() => {
118	    if (inheritedSettings && shotId && status === 'ready') {
119	      if (!hasShotSettings) {
120	        saveImmediate(inheritedSettings).catch(err => {
121	          normalizeAndPresentError(err, { context: 'useJoinSegmentsSettings', showToast: false });
122	        });
123	      }
124	    }
125	  }, [hasShotSettings, inheritedSettings, saveImmediate, shotId, status]);
126	  
127	  // Persist settings to localStorage for future inheritance
128	  useEffect(() => {
129	    if (shotId && projectId && status === 'ready' && settings) {
130	      try {
131	        // Project-specific key
132	        const [REDACTED](projectId);
133	        localStorage.setItem(projectStorageKey, JSON.stringify(settings));
134	        
135	        // Global key (for cross-project inheritance)
136	        // Clear prompt when inheriting to new project (shot-specific)
137	        const globalSettings = { ...settings, prompt: '', negativePrompt: '' };
138	        localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEYS.GLOBAL_LAST_ACTIVE_JOIN_SEGMENTS_SETTINGS, JSON.stringify(globalSettings));
139	      } catch (e) {
140	        normalizeAndPresentError(e, { context: 'useJoinSegmentsSettings', showToast: false });
141	      }
142	    }
143	  }, [settings, shotId, projectId, status]);
144	  
145	  // Memoize return value
146	  return useMemo(() => ({
147	    settings,
148	    status: status as 'idle' | 'loading' | 'ready' | 'saving' | 'error',
149	    shotId: entityId,

> AGENT

Found it. Diagnosis:

**Root cause**: In `useAutoSaveSettings.ts:309`, the store is seeded with `authoritativeSettings ?? defaults`. If the shot's DB row was saved before `selectedLoras` was added to the defaults (it's a relatively new field), the RQ-returned settings object is missing that key. The store bootstraps with the partial object, and `localEntity.settings.selectedLoras` is `undefined`. Then `joinSelectedLoras.length > 0` at `useJoinSegmentsSetup.ts:233` throws.

**Fix**: merge defaults into the seed so missing keys fall back. One-line change:

> TOOL

tool_use Edit
```json
{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/shared/settings/hooks/useAutoSaveSettings.ts",
  "old_string": "    const seed = cloneValue(authoritativeSettings ?? defaults);",
  "new_string": "    const seed = cloneValue({ ...defaults, ...(authoritativeSettings ?? {}) } as T);"
}
```

> TOOL

tool_result Edit
```
The file /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/shared/settings/hooks/useAutoSaveSettings.ts has been updated successfully.
```

> AGENT

Save and reload the `/tools/travel-between-images` page. The error should be gone. Any shot whose DB row predates a defaults-schema addition (including `selectedLoras`, `stitchAfterGenerate`, `generateMode`) will now get the missing fields backfilled from defaults.

Note: this is a M1a regression — the old `useAutoSaveSettings` probably merged defaults implicitly. The gate/review didn't catch it because `useJoinSegmentsSettings.ts` isn't in M1a's enumerated migration list (it's not a direct `useAutoSaveSettings` caller through the primary path they tested). M1b and M1c reviews didn't touch this code path.

If the page now loads, we should:
1. Check other consumers for the same pattern (any settings hook whose defaults grew over time)
2. Commit this fix as `fix: merge defaults into settings seed so partial DB rows don't surface undefined fields` on top of the m1b-m1c-stores branch

Want me to grep for other vulnerable callers?

> AGENT

Scan complete. Summary:

**Fixed by the one-line change in `createEntityStore.getSeed`**: all 20 `useAutoSaveSettings` callers. Any consumer destructuring `settings.whatever` will now get defaults-merged objects, so missing DB fields fall back to defaults regardless of crash pattern (`.length`, `.map`, `.filter`, direct value reads).

**Callers I checked for the specific crash pattern**:
- `useJoinSegmentsSetup.ts:233` ← original crash, fixed ✓
- `useJoinClipsGenerate.ts`, `JoinClipsPage.tsx`, `useReplaceMode.ts`, `VideoTravelSettingsProvider.tsx` — destructure `settings.settings`, covered by the getSeed fix
- `useVideoEditing.ts`, `usePromptRouting.ts` — already defensive (`|| ''`, `|| []`), safe regardless
- Direct `useToolSettings` callers (`VideoEditorPage`, `AgentChatContext`, `ToolsPane`, `useHomeNavigation`, `useAddToVideoEditor`) — read scalars only, no array ops, bypass the store but also don't hit the crash pattern

**Net**: one change in one file covers everything. Root cause was that `getSeed` returned `db ?? lastUsed ?? defaults` — picking ONE source rather than merging. Partial DB rows (from schema-growth over time) surfaced `undefined` fields. Now it spreads defaults underneath whichever source is chosen.

Reload `/tools/travel-between-images` and the error should be gone. Want me to commit both files onto `megaplan/m1b-m1c-stores` and push?

   - Scroll the timeline → `TimelineCanvas` ≤ 2 renders per scroll

Only then open the PR. Shipping an unverified-but-"approved" refactor to main is how "worked on my CI" regressions happen.

To queue them I need to settle two things with you first:

**1. Which milestones?** Per the 2-week metaplan's Day 10 + plan.md, the defined-but-not-run milestones are:

- **M2** (overlay stack) — conditional on smoke test; scoped to Base UI portal primitives
- **M3** (realtime events store) — `RealtimeProvider` + task hooks → store
- **M4** (panes + context audit) — `PanesContext` → store + lint rule
- **M5** (test triage) — delete implementation-coupled tests after refactor
- **M6** (behavior tests) — RTL user-interaction tests + 2 E2E regression tests

I'd recommend **M3 → M4** as the next back-to-back chain (both are domain-store migrations, reuse the same `createEntityStore` pattern, independent surfaces). M2 is conditional — skip unless smoke test fails. M5/M6 are cleanup, different character, better run after merge.

**2. Who writes the idea files?** None of `/workspace/ideas/M3*.txt`, `M4*.txt` etc. exist yet. Options:

- **You draft** and I help edit.
- **Skip idea files** — feed plan.md sections directly to `megaplan init`. Faster but loses the brief-tailoring benefits.

My recommendation: **I draft M3 + M4 idea files** (with the briefing rules applied), show you for review, then I update the watchdog cron prompt to chain `M3 → M4`, kick it off, and the existing 2h watchdog handles the rest. ~15-20 min to get chain running.

One concern: we've hit **zero smoke-test verification** of the original lightbox-textarea bug yet. If the refactor subtly broke that bug and we pile M3+M4 on top, we'll have to untangle more. Suggest: **run the Model Select / textarea test first (5 min), then queue M3+M4.**

> DEVELOPER

I already yested the model select and textarea, why skip M2 (overlay stack)?

> AGENT

Because plan.md explicitly designed M2 to be skipped if the motivating bugs are gone — it's the highest-regression-risk milestone in the whole chain (wrapping ~20 Base UI portal callsites, centralizing `body.pointer-events` ownership, focus restoration). Running it without a reproducible symptom trades real regression risk for speculative hygiene.

That said — "skip" ≠ "never." If you want portal ownership cleaned up as an architectural improvement regardless of whether the bug exists, M2 is legit work. The question is priority: is *the next week* better spent on M2 (hardening) or M3+M4 (continuing the domain-store pattern into realtime + panes)?

Tradeoff: M2 = bug-insurance, touches many files, easier regressions. M3+M4 = more domain migrations, each independently useful, reuses proven `createEntityStore`.

Your call — want M2 included in the queue after M3/M4, or still skip it?

> DEVELOPER

But it doing M2 better engineering? Will it prevent bugs in the future if we get it right?

> AGENT

Honest answer: **yes, done right, M2 is better engineering.**

The render storm was the *trigger* of the Model Select flash-close. The *mechanism* — Base UI portal unmounts racing with document-level click-outside detection — still exists across ~20 portal callsites. Any future component that causes a render during an overlay's initial mount (new feature, new hook, third-party lib upgrade) can retrigger the same class of bug through a different trigger. M1→M1c eliminated the known triggers; M2 would harden the mechanism itself.

Three concrete future bugs M2 prevents:
1. **Body `pointer-events: none` ownership races** when overlays stack (A opens → B opens → A closes first → B is still open but body was restored).
2. **Focus restoration in wrong order** when overlays close out of order (accessibility regression, rarely caught in tests).
3. **z-index drift** as new portal types land without a stack to consult.

The catch: "done right" is load-bearing. Plan.md budgeted 3-5 days; this chain compressed 2 weeks into 48 hours with force-proceeds and unverified criteria. If M2 gets the same compressed treatment, the wrappers diverge from Base UI's API over time and M2 becomes net-negative. Budget it at the full pace plan.md designed it for.

Also: M2 delivers value **regardless of whether the current bug stays fixed.** M3+M4 only reduce render storms in domains that may not have had bad storms to begin with. M2 hardens the overlay stack universally. Strong case for running it.

Revised recommendation: **queue M2 → M3 → M4**, in that order. M2 first because it's the highest-risk milestone and deserves fresh attention, M3/M4 are lower-risk extensions of proven patterns.

#!/bin/bash
# Thin shim around `megaplan chain`.
#
# The real chain logic now lives in megaplan itself (`megaplan chain`,
# introduced in v0.16.0). That command reads a YAML spec, drives each
# milestone via the same auto-loop used by `megaplan auto`, and persists
# progress to chain_state.json next to the spec. Plan-state decisions
# (next_step, needs_rework, stall detection, escalation) stay in megaplan
# where they belong — bash no longer has to replicate them.
#
# Usage:
#   /workspace/chain.sh                           # uses /workspace/chain.yaml
#   /workspace/chain.sh /path/to/other-spec.yaml
#
# Copy chain.yaml.example to /workspace/chain.yaml and edit before first run.

set -u

SPEC="${1:-/workspace/chain.yaml}"
LOG=/workspace/chain.log
exec > >(tee -a "$LOG") 2>&1

echo "[chain $(date -Iseconds)] starting megaplan chain --spec $SPEC"

if [[ ! -f "$SPEC" ]]; then
  echo "[chain] spec not found: $SPEC"
  echo "[chain] copy chain.yaml.example to $SPEC and edit before running."
  exit 1
fi

cd /workspace/reigh-app || { echo "[chain] repo missing"; exit 1; }

exec megaplan chain --spec "$SPEC"
```

  "content": "M2 — Overlay stack. Centralize Base UI portal ownership to harden against the class of bugs that caused the Model Select flash-close (and will re-emerge through other triggers if the mechanism isn't hardened).\n\n## Executor rules\n\nEXECUTOR HARD RULE (authoritative): For any task whose satisfaction requires a browser, runtime app instance, device, or manual human input that the sandboxed executor cannot produce (examples: telemetry overlay capture from a running app, drag/resize smoke checks, keyboard/playback behavior, AgentChat bridge manual test, pending-add URL flow verification, Model Select click behavior, textarea typing verification, any rendering count measured at runtime) — return status=skipped on FIRST encounter with executor_notes evidence naming the blocker (e.g. \"no browser-driving path available\"). DO NOT return status=blocked. DO NOT retry the task expecting different sandbox capabilities. The review/gate phase already marks these criteria as deferred_human; skipped tasks are first-class evidence that the criterion is deferred to post-milestone human verification. Blocked retries waste iteration budget and do not change outcome.\n\n## Motivation\n\nM1 → M1c eliminated the known render-storm triggers that caused Base UI overlay portals to unmount before their click-outside detection settled. That fixed the immediate Model Select / textarea-typing bugs. But the underlying mechanism — ad-hoc ownership of `body.pointer-events: none`, per-portal focus restoration, and uncoordinated z-index stacking across ~20 portal primitives — still exists. Any future render-storm trigger (new hook, new provider, new lib upgrade) can re-express the same bug class.\n\nM2 hardens the mechanism itself: centralize overlay lifecycle so bugs in this class have one place to fix instead of twenty.\n\n## Goal\n\nBuild a single overlay-stack store that wraps every Base UI portal primitive and owns:\n1. `document.body.style.pointerEvents` — only the outermost open overlay sets it to 'none'; close order does not matter.\n2. Focus restoration — central stack tracks which element had focus at overlay open; restores on close in reverse order.\n3. Z-index layering — overlays consult the stack for their layer; no hardcoded z-index across files.\n\n## Scope\n\n### In scope\n\n- `src/shared/state/overlayStack.ts` — Zustand store (using `createEntityStore` factory pattern where appropriate, or a simpler slice if entity-keyed isn't a fit).\n- Wrapper components in `src/shared/components/ui/overlay/` for each Base UI portal primitive:\n  - Dialog, Popover, Select, Menu, Tooltip-with-modal, Drawer, AlertDialog, ContextMenu\n- Migrate every callsite to the wrapper. Inventory command:\n  `rg 'from .*@base-ui/react/(dialog|popover|select|menu|tooltip|alert-dialog|context-menu|popup)' src/ --type ts -l`\n  Expect 20+ callsites; actual count must match inventory at milestone start.\n\n### Out of scope\n\n- Replacing Base UI itself.\n- Adding new overlay types.\n- Touching overlay styling beyond what centralization requires.\n- Writing E2E tests (no browser harness in repo).\n\n## Dependencies (technical, not temporal)\n\n- The M1-M1c store substrate must exist in the worktree: specifically `src/shared/state/createEntityStore.ts` and `src/shared/state/selectionStore.ts`. Verify at milestone start via `ls src/shared/state/`.\n- React 18+ (already in repo).\n- Base UI portal primitives (already in repo).\n\n## Implementation approach\n\n1. **Inventory** all portal callsites via the grep above. Document each with its current z-index, focus-restoration behavior (if any), and pointer-events ownership.\n2. **Build `overlayStack.ts`** — store with actions: `pushOverlay({ id, type, restoreFocusTo })`, `popOverlay(id)`, `getTopOverlay()`. Selector: `useIsOverlayTopmost(id)`.\n3. **Build wrapper components** — one per primitive. Each wrapper:\n   - Calls `pushOverlay` on mount; `popOverlay` on unmount.\n   - Reads `useIsOverlayTopmost(id)` to decide whether to own body pointer-events.\n   - Uses stack-layered z-index via a CSS variable pegged to stack depth.\n4. **Migrate callsites** — one primitive type at a time. Keep prop API identical so consumer code doesn't change. Delete unused per-callsite z-index overrides and focus-management code as wrappers land.\n5. **Regression harness** — integration tests (RTL/Vitest) that open/close overlay sequences and assert `document.body.style.pointerEvents === ''` after all overlays close.\n\n## MUST (all automated)\n\n- `npx tsc --noEmit` clean on the branch.\n- `npm test` passes at M0/M1c baseline count or higher.\n- No portal primitive imports remain outside `src/shared/components/ui/overlay/` except in the wrappers themselves. Verify: `rg 'from .*@base-ui/react/(dialog|popover|select|menu|tooltip|alert-dialog|context-menu|popup)' src/ --type ts -l | grep -v 'ui/overlay/'` returns empty.\n- Integration tests covering open-A → open-B → close-A → close-B sequence for at least Dialog+Popover and Menu+Dialog pairings. Asserts `document.body.style.pointerEvents === ''` after full close.\n- `overlayStack.ts` exports a public API documented with JSDoc on each action.\n- No `z-index: [number]` literals in any migrated overlay wrapper file; all layering flows through the stack.\n\n## DEFERRED_HUMAN (skipped by executor, verified manually post-milestone)\n\n- Nested overlay sequences: LoRA modal → Model Select inside it; close LoRA → Model Select should close cleanly with no focus stolen, no body pointer-events stuck.\n- Drag-while-overlay-open: open any overlay, drag a clip in timeline — overlay stays open, drag works, no click-outside false-positive dismissing overlay.\n- Tab key traversal inside overlay stays trapped; Escape closes topmost; focus returns to opener.\n\n## Non-goals / explicit choices\n\n- No migration of non-Base-UI overlays (our own bespoke modals) unless they already use the same portal plumbing.\n- No accessibility audit beyond focus-restoration correctness (different milestone if needed).\n- No animation changes — this milestone is plumbing, not polish.\n\n## Risk notes\n\n- 20+ callsite migration is the largest-surface milestone in the chain. If a wrapper diverges from Base UI's prop API, all consumers of that primitive break. Keep wrappers thin.\n- `document.body.style.pointerEvents` is a shared global; any non-migrated Base UI primitive opened during this milestone could conflict with the stack. Recommend landing all primitives in one PR.\n"
}
